                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.22.8a"><l> atha sarvāśrāvam vakṣyāmaḥ ||
                            tatrāghṛṣṭāsu<unclear>c</unclear>chinnāsu vā tvaksu

                        bhṛśam upācaret | yatra <cb n="2"/> saṃrambho vedanāvān bhavati |
                        tatra śalyañ jānīyāt | sandhipraṇaṣṭe snehasvedopapannāṃ
                            sandhīprasāraṇākuñcanabandhana<sic ana="symbol, perhaps a filler"/>pīḍanaiḥ <cb n="3"/> bhṛśam
                        upācaret | yatra saṃrambho vedanāvān bhavati | tatra śalyam
                        vijānīyāt | marmapraṇaṣṭe tv ananyabhāvā<lb n="4"/>tmarmaṇāmuktaṃ
                        parīkṣaṇaṃ bhavati | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.13"><l> sāmānyalakṣaṇam api
                            hastiskandhāśvapṛṣṭhaparvatadrumārohaṇadru<cb n="2"/>tayānala<unclear>ṅgha</unclear>naplavanavyāyāmajṛmbhodgarakṣāvaṣuṣṭhīvanahasanaprāṇāyāmair
                        vātamūtrapurīṣasukrotsarggair vā yatra vedanā vā bhavati | tatra
                            <cb n="3"/> śalyaṃ jānīyāt || </l></lg>





                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.26.15"><l> bhavanti cātra | āturaś cāpi yaṃ deśam
                        abhīkṣṇaṃ pa<unclear>ri</unclear>rakṣati |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.29.19"><l> navamyām atha ṣaṣṭhyām vā pakṣasandhidineṣu vā
                        ||
                        <caesura/>
                         vaidyaṃ ya upasarpanti dūtās te cāpi garhitāḥ | </l></lg>

                        <cb n="1"/>uṣṇagātro 'tigātrañ ca yaḥ śītena pravepate |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.18cd"><l>
                         dhūmanīhāravāsobhir āvṛtām iva medinīm | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.19"><l> pra<cb n="1"/>dīptam iva lokañ ca yo vāplutam
                        ivāmbhasā |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhūmim aṣṭāpadākārāṃ lekhābhir yaś ca paśyati | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.23"><l> yo vā mayūrakaṇṭhābham vidhū<cb n="2"/>maṃ
                        vahnim īkṣate ||
                        <caesura/>
                         dhruvam ākāśagaṅgām vā tam bruvanti ga<pb n="26r" facs="29"/><lb n="1"/>tāyuṣaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.21"><l> yo <unclear reason="faded">jyotsnādarśatoyeṣu</unclear> svacchāyāṃ ca na paśyati |
                        <caesura/>
                         paśyaty ekāṅgahīnam vā vikṛtām vānyasatvajāṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.22"><l> śvakā<cb n="1"/>kakaṅkagṛdhrāṇāṃ pretānāṃ
                        yakṣarākṣasāṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         piśācoraganāgānāṃ bhūtānāṃ vikṛtān api | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.22.add1"><l> svasthaḥ sa labhate vyādhiṃ vyādhito
                        mṛtyu <cb n="2"/>gacchati || 0 || vraṇapraśnaṃ vraṇāsrāvaṃ
                        kṛtyākṛtyavidhiṃ tathā | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.22.add2"><l> vyādhyuddeśīyam adhyā<unclear reason="illegible">yaṃ śa</unclear>strakarmāṣṭakan ta<lb n="2"/>thā || praṇaṣṭaśalyavijñānaṃ śalyāpanayanam eva ca | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.22.add3"><l> viparītavraṇajñānaṃ dūtasvapnaviparyayam
                        | pañce<cb n="1"/>ndriyārthavibhrāntiṃ proktaṃ vai tṛtīyo daśaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.30.trailer"><l>|| 30 || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.1"><l> athātaḥ cchāyāvipratipattim adhyāyaṃ
                        vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.3"><l> śyāvā lohitikā nī<cb n="2"/>ḥ <unclear reason="faded">pī</unclear>tikā vāpi dehinām |
                        <caesura/>
                         abhidravanti yaṃ cchāyā sa parāsur asaṃsayaṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.4"><l> hrīr apakrāmati yataḥ kā<lb n="3"/><unclear reason="faded">ntismṛtidhṛtiśriyaḥ</unclear> |
                        <caesura/>
                         akasmād yaṃ bhajante ca sa parāsur asaṃśayaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.5"><l> yasyādharauṣṭhaṃ patitaḥ kṣiptaś corddhan
                            tatho<cb n="1"/>ttaraḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ubhau vā jāmbavābhāsau sa parāsur asaṃśayam | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.6"><l> āraktā daśanā yasya syāvā va syuḥ patanti vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         khāñjanapratibhā vā<cb n="2"/>pi taṅ gatāyuṣam ādiśet | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.7"><l> kṛṣṇā snigdhāvaliptā jihvā gunā ca yasya vai |
                        <caesura/>
                         karkaśā ca bhaved yasya so <lb n="4"/>cirād vijahāty asūn | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.8"><l> kuṭilā sphuṭitā vāpi śūnā vā yasya nāsikā |
                        <caesura/>
                         bhagnā vā sphurate vāpi sa pa<cb n="1"/>rāsur asaṃsayaḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.9"><l> saṃkṣipte viṣame stabdhe rakte supte ca locane
                        |
                        <caesura/>
                         yasya syātāṃ śrute vāpi sa parāsu asaṃśayaṃ | </l></lg>





                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.11"><l> na dhārayati yaḥ <cb n="2"/>śīrṣan nāharanty
                        annam āsyagaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         ekāgradṛṣṭimūḍhātmā sa parāsu yasaṃśayaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.12"><l> balavān durbalo vāpi saṃ<lb n="5"/>mohaṃ yo
                            <unclear cert="medium" reason="faded">ddhi</unclear>gacchati |
                        <caesura/>
                         utthāpyamāno bahusa<unclear reason="illegible">ḥ sa</unclear>
                        parāsur asaṃ śayaḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.13"><l> uttānaḥ sarvadā śete pādau <cb n="1"/>vikurute
                        ca yaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         viprasāraṇaśīlo vā sa parāsur asaṃśayaḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.14"><l> śītapādakarocchvāsaḥ cchinnāsvāsaś ca yo
                        bhavet |
                        <caesura/>
                         kākocchvā<cb n="2"/>saś ca yo martyaḥ sa parāsur asaṃśayaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.15"><l> nidrā na cchidyate yasya yo vā jāgartti
                        sarvadā |
                        <caesura/>
                         muhyed vā vaktukā<lb n="6"/>maś ca pratyākhyeyaḥ sa jānatā | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.16"><l> parilihed uttaroṣṭham uṅgarāṃś ca karoti vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         pretair vā bhāṣate sārdhaṃ sa parā<cb n="1"/>sur asaṃśayaḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.17"><l> khebhyaḥ saromakūpebhyo yasya raktaṃ
                        pravarttate |
                        <caesura/>
                         puruṣasyāviṣārttasya sa parāsur asaṃśayaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.18"><l> vātāṣṭhīlā <cb n="2"/>tu hṛdaye yasyorddham
                        anuyāyinī |
                        <caesura/>
                         rujānnavidveṣakarī sa parāsur asaṃśayaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.19"><l> ananyopadravakṛtaḥ pā<lb n="7"/>daḥ
                        śophasamutthitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         puruṣaṃ hanti nārīñ ca mukhajo guhyajo dvayaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.20"><l> atīsāro jvaro dhmānañ chardiḥ <cb n="1"/>s<unclear reason="faded">ū</unclear>nāṅgameḍhratā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kāsina svāsino vāpi yasya taṃ kṣīṇam ādiśet | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.21"><l> svedo dāhaś ca balavāṃ hikkā svāsaś ca
                            mānava<unclear cert="medium" reason="faded">ṃ</unclear> |
                        <caesura/>
                         bala<cb n="2"/>vantam api prāṇair viyujanti na saṃśaya<unclear cert="medium" reason="faded">ṃ</unclear> | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.22"><l> syāvā jihvā bhaved yasya savyañ cākṣi
                        nimajjati |
                        <caesura/>
                         mukhañ ca <pb n="26v" facs="29"/><lb n="1"/><!-- A45-05+73274+029.jpg --><!-- A1267-11+103612+063.jpg -->jāyate
                        pūtir yasya taṃ parivarjayet | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.23"><l> netre cāsyena pūryete svidyete caraṇau tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         cakṣuś cākulatāṃ yāti ya<cb n="1"/>marāṣṭraṃ gamiṣyataḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.24"><l> atimātra laghūni syur gātrāṇy atigurūṇi vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         yasyākasmāt sa vijñeyo gantā caiva yamālayaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.25"><l> paṅkama<cb n="2"/>tsyavasātailaghṛtagandhāṃś
                        ca ye narāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         piṣṭagandhāṃś ca y<subst><del rend="erased">o</del>
                            <add>e</add></subst> vānti gatās te yamasādanaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.26"><l> yūkā lalāṭam āyā<lb n="2"/>nti balin nāśnānti
                        vāyasāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yeṣāñ cāpi ratiṃ nāsti gatās te yamasādanaṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.27"><l> jvarātīsāraśophā syur yasyā<cb n="1"/>nyonyāvasādinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakṣīṇañ ca balaṃ yasya na sa śakya cikitsituṃ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.28"><l> kṣīṇasya yasya kṣuttṛṣṇe hṛdyair mṛṣṭair
                        hitais tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         annapānai<cb n="2"/>r na śāsyeta tasya mṛtyur upasthitaḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.29"><l> pravāhikā śiraḥśūlaṅ koṣṭhaśūlaś ca dāruṇam |
                        <caesura/>
                         pipāsā balahāniś ca <lb n="3"/>tasya mṛtyur upasthitaḥ | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.30"><l> viṣameṇopacāreṇa karmabhiś ca purākṛtaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         anityāc ca jaṃtūnāṃ jīvitan nidha<cb n="1"/>nam vrajet | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.31"><l> pretā dūtā piśācāś ca rakṣāṃsi vividhāni ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         maraṇīyan naran nityam upasarpanti sarvadā | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.32"><l> tāni bheṣajavīryāṇi prati<cb n="2"/>nighnanti
                        sarvadā |
                        <caesura/>
